# Personal engineering and communication preferences

## Work and evidence
- Surface material uncertainty. Use reasonable, reversible assumptions for routine choices; ask when a consequential decision or required information is missing.
- Solve the requested problem with the smallest coherent change. Preserve unrelated work and existing conventions.
- Read enough surrounding code and its callers to understand an edit. Read the whole file when changing its structure, rewriting it, or when a narrow excerpt leaves ownership or behavior unclear.
- Define observable completion criteria. Match verification to the changed behavior and repository policy; report what the evidence proves and what still needs a live check.
- Reuse authorization already given. Continue independent authorized work while a real question is pending. Preserve explicit read-only boundaries and separate authorization for merge, push, publication, and release when the project requires it.

## Communication
- Lead with the answer or outcome. Use plain English for a smart reader who may not know the implementation details.
- Keep paragraphs and bullets short. Choose prose, a list, or a table to fit the information; do not force every response into a template.
- Explain necessary technical terms naturally. Include file, command, or API evidence when it helps assess a claim.
- Give useful progress updates during substantial work according to the host's cadence. Finals report results, validation, and material remaining work.
- Avoid preambles, padding, em dashes, and closing offers that do not identify a real decision.
- Ask the smallest useful set of questions; reuse answers already supplied. When something fails, explain the effect and the next concrete action without dumping irrelevant logs.
- These chat preferences do not limit the length or structure of a requested document, draft, script, or other artifact.

## Skill selection and local overrides
Invoke a skill for a matching task or an explicit user request. A skill's claim that it must recruit itself into every task, or a numeric likelihood threshold, does not establish relevance. Apply only the procedures needed for the current task.

 154: In `skills/multi-model-verify/references/model-prompting-notes.md`, insert the following block immediately BEFORE the line `## The scope guard (every brief, every lane)`. The eight lines between the markers must be byte-identical to Step 3's pin, each on one physical line.
 155: 
 156: ```markdown
 157: ## Artifact roots (every path a round writes)
 158: 
 159: THE single source for where a debate lands in the reviewed repository,
 160: in the same swap-by-one-edit shape as the model declarations above.
 161: `tools/artifact-roots.ps1` parses the eight lines below at runtime and
 162: fails loud when one is missing; nothing else in the plugin names a
 163: round root by hand, and `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py`
 164: sweeps the plugin surface for one that does. Item 100 (2026-09-12) is
 165: the record of why: one consumer repository held rounds, ledgers and a
 166: mirror under four roots, because each writer read the repo-side
 167: override on its own.
 168: 
 169: <!-- contract:start id=artifact-roots -->
 170: Canonical docs root: `docs/superpowers`
 171: Canonical docs root override: `dev/docs/superpowers`
 172: Canonical frozen plan path: `<docs-root>/plans/<date>-<topic>.md`
 173: Canonical rounds root: `<docs-root>/plans/rounds/<date>-<topic>/`
 174: Canonical SDD ledger root: `.superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>/`
 175: Canonical review mirror root: `<TEMP>/<short-name>/`
 176: Canonical attestation root: `<git-common-dir>/parallax/attestations/`
 177: Canonical checkpoint root: `<git-common-dir>/parallax/application-checkpoints/`
 178: <!-- contract:end -->
 179: 
 180: The override rule, applied to exactly the rows that carry
 181: `<docs-root>`: the docs root is the override value when that directory
 182: exists under the repo root, else the default; `-DocsRoot` names it
 183: explicitly and wins over both, and the tool prints which of the three
 184: fired as `docs-root source`. A repository carrying both directories
 185: resolves to the override silently, which is what the printed source
 186: line is for.
 187: 
 188: The other rows are FIXED, each for a reason the row cannot carry:
 189: 
 190: - SDD ledger: Superpowers owns it. Its subagent-driven-development
 191:   `scripts/sdd-workspace` creates the directory and its self-ignoring
 192:   `.gitignore`, and its ledger check reads `<workspace>/progress.md`
 193:   back, so the plugin cites the path and never relocates it.
 194: - Review mirror: never inside the reviewed repository.
 195:   `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1` refuses a path equal to, inside, or
 196:   containing the repo; `<TEMP>` is the controller host's temp
 197:   directory, and references/preflight-mirror.md owns the short-name
 198:   and path-budget rules.
 199: - Attestation and checkpoint: under the git COMMON dir, so recording a
 200:   verdict cannot move `HEAD` out from under its own SHA and every
 201:   worktree sees one record; `tools/verify-attestation.ps1` re-hashes the
 202:   checkpoint there. `<git-common-dir>` is what
 203:   `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` prints, which in a linked worktree is
 204:   not `.git`.
 205: 
 206: The operating rule, which SKILL.md's preflight step 4 points at:
 207: 
 208: - Run the tool once per debate, before round 1, against the reviewed
 209:   repository (the REAL repo, not the mirror: the mirror is where the
 210:   reviewer reads, the repo is where the record lands). Write its output
 211:   to session scratch OUTSIDE the repository, beside the briefs. It is a
 212:   retained artifact: it enters the rounds root as `artifact-roots.txt`
 213:   when the other round files do, after the last wrapper exits, so the
 214:   quiet period in references/preflight-mirror.md is never touched.
 215: - Every later act that names one of these paths uses the printed value:
 216:   the frozen plan save, the rounds retention, the ledger path handed to
 217:   agents/fable-reviewer.md, the attestation the emitter is expected to
 218:   write.
 219: - Before the retention copy, run the tool with `-Assert <destination>`;
 220:   exit 0 is the only clean answer. The ledger and mirror rows are not
 221:   in the assert set, because the session never copies into them.
 222: - Dispatch directories, receipts, briefs, prior-state files and the
 223:   probe's override file are session scratch outside the repository for
 224:   the whole round; only their retained copies enter the rounds root.
 225: - A controller other than Claude Code is outside this contract. The
 226:   2026-09-07 record in item 100 is of one that wrote a 54 MB copy of a
 227:   worktree under a root of its own naming; the plugin binds its own
 228:   tools and the prose the Claude controller follows, not a foreign one.
 229: 
 230: ```
 231: 
 232: (The block ends with one blank line so the existing `## The scope guard` heading keeps a blank line above it.)

 251: ---
 252: 
 253: ### Task 2: The resolver tool
 254: 
 255: **Files:**
 256: - Create: `tools/artifact-roots.ps1`
 257: - Modify: `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py` (append the resolver group)
 258: 
 259: **Interfaces:**
 260: - Consumes: the `artifact-roots` region and its eight labels from Task 1.
 261: - Produces: `tools/artifact-roots.ps1 -RepoRoot <path> [-DocsRoot <rel>] [-Assert <path>] [-Json]`. Text output is nine `name: value` lines in this order: `repo`, `docs-root`, `docs-root source`, `frozen-plan`, `rounds`, `sdd-ledger`, `review-mirror`, `attestation`, `checkpoint`, then with `-Assert` one line `assert: inside <root name>: <path>` or `assert: outside every retained root: <path>`. JSON output is one object with keys `repo`, `docsRoot`, `source`, `frozenPlan`, `rounds`, `sddLedger`, `reviewMirror`, `attestation`, `checkpoint`, and with `-Assert` an `assert` object `{path, inside, root}`. Exit 0 resolved or asserted inside; 1 asserted outside; 2 parameter fault, unreadable declaration, or not a git tree. Task 4 calls `-Assert`.
 262: 
 263: - [ ] **Step 1: Append the resolver tests**
 264: 
 265: Append to `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py`:
 266: 
 267: ```python
 268: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 269: # Group 3a: the resolver
 270: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 271: def run_resolver(*args, tool=None):
 272:     return subprocess.run(
 273:         [POWERSHELL, "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File",
 274:          str(tool or TOOL), *args],
 275:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
 276: 
 277: 
 278: def git(repo, *args):
 279:     return subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(repo), *args],
 280:                           capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
 281: 
 282: 
 283: def make_repo(tmp_path, name="repo", commits=1):
 284:     repo = tmp_path / name
 285:     repo.mkdir()
 286:     git(tmp_path, "init", "-q", str(repo))
 287:     for i in range(commits):
 288:         (repo / f"file{i}.txt").write_text(f"content {i}\n")
 289:         git(repo, "add", f"file{i}.txt")
 290:         git(repo, "-c", "user.email=t@t", "-c", "user.name=t",
 291:             "commit", "-q", "-m", f"commit {i}")
 292:     return repo
 293: 
 294: 
 295: def norm(p):
 296:     """Forward slashes, no trailing separator, case-folded: Windows
 297:     paths compare case-insensitively and the tool prints forward
 298:     slashes."""
 299:     return str(p).replace("\\", "/").rstrip("/").lower()
 300: 
 301: 
 302: def resolved(repo, *extra):
 303:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), *extra, "-Json")
 304:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 305:     return json.loads(proc.stdout)
 306: 
 307: 
 308: @needs_host
 309: def test_resolver_prints_the_default_set(tmp_path):
 310:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 311:     got = resolved(repo)
 312:     assert got["source"] == "default"
 313:     assert norm(got["repo"]) == norm(repo)
 314:     assert norm(got["docsRoot"]) == norm(repo / "docs/superpowers")
 315:     assert norm(got["frozenPlan"]) == norm(
 316:         repo / "docs/superpowers/plans/<date>-<topic>.md")
 317:     assert norm(got["rounds"]) == norm(
 318:         repo / "docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-<topic>")
 319:     assert norm(got["sddLedger"]) == norm(
 320:         repo / ".superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>")
 321:     assert norm(got["attestation"]) == norm(
 322:         repo / ".git/parallax/attestations")
 323:     assert norm(got["checkpoint"]) == norm(
 324:         repo / ".git/parallax/application-checkpoints")
 325:     # The mirror row resolves OUTSIDE the repo, under the host temp dir.
 326:     assert norm(got["reviewMirror"]).endswith("/<short-name>")
 327:     assert not norm(got["reviewMirror"]).startswith(norm(repo) + "/")
 328: 
 329: 
 330: @needs_host
 331: def test_resolver_text_output_names_each_root_then_the_source(tmp_path):
 332:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 333:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo))
 334:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 335:     keys = [ln.split(": ", 1)[0] for ln in proc.stdout.splitlines()
 336:             if ": " in ln]
 337:     assert keys == ["repo", "docs-root", "docs-root source", "frozen-plan",
 338:                     "rounds", "sdd-ledger", "review-mirror", "attestation",
 339:                     "checkpoint"]
 340: 
 341: 
 342: @needs_host
 343: def test_override_directory_selects_the_override_root(tmp_path):
 344:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 345:     (repo / "dev" / "docs" / "superpowers").mkdir(parents=True)
 346:     got = resolved(repo)
 347:     assert got["source"] == "override directory exists"
 348:     assert norm(got["rounds"]) == norm(
 349:         repo / "dev/docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-<topic>")
 350:     assert norm(got["frozenPlan"]) == norm(
 351:         repo / "dev/docs/superpowers/plans/<date>-<topic>.md")
 352:     # Fixed rows do not move with the docs root.
 353:     assert norm(got["sddLedger"]) == norm(
 354:         repo / ".superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>")
 355:     assert norm(got["attestation"]) == norm(
 356:         repo / ".git/parallax/attestations")
 357: 
 358: 
 359: @needs_host
 360: def test_docsroot_argument_wins_over_both_rules(tmp_path):
 361:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 362:     (repo / "dev" / "docs" / "superpowers").mkdir(parents=True)
 363:     got = resolved(repo, "-DocsRoot", "other/root")
 364:     assert got["source"] == "-DocsRoot"
 365:     assert norm(got["frozenPlan"]) == norm(
 366:         repo / "other/root/plans/<date>-<topic>.md")
 367: 
 368: 
 369: @needs_host
 370: @pytest.mark.parametrize("bad", ["../x", "a/../b", "C:/abs/root", "/rooted"])
 371: def test_docsroot_refuses_escapes_and_rooted_values(tmp_path, bad):
 372:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 373:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-DocsRoot", bad)
 374:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 375:     assert proc.stdout.startswith("ERROR:"), proc.stdout
 376: 
 377: 
 378: @needs_host
 379: def test_reporoot_must_be_a_git_working_tree(tmp_path):
 380:     plain = tmp_path / "plain"
 381:     plain.mkdir()
 382:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(plain))
 383:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 384:     assert "not a git" in proc.stdout
 385: 
 386: 
 387: @needs_host
 388: def test_missing_declaration_line_is_an_error_not_a_default(tmp_path):
 389:     # The tool finds the notes relative to its own location, so a copy
 390:     # of the tool beside a doctored copy of the notes exercises the
 391:     # parse failure without touching the real declaration.
 392:     fake = tmp_path / "plugin"
 393:     (fake / "tools").mkdir(parents=True)
 394:     notes_dir = fake / "skills" / "multi-model-verify" / "references"
 395:     notes_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
 396:     shutil.copy(TOOL, fake / "tools" / "artifact-roots.ps1")
 397:     doctored = read(NOTES).replace(
 398:         "Canonical rounds root: `<docs-root>/plans/rounds/<date>-<topic>/`\n", "")
 399:     assert doctored != read(NOTES)
 400:     (notes_dir / "model-prompting-notes.md").write_text(doctored, encoding="utf-8")
 401:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 402:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo),
 403:                         tool=fake / "tools" / "artifact-roots.ps1")
 404:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 405:     assert "Canonical rounds root" in proc.stdout
 406: 
 407: 
 408: @needs_host
 409: @pytest.mark.parametrize("rel, expect", [
 410:     ("docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/2026-09-12-x/brief.md", 0),
 411:     ("docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-12-x.md", 0),
 412:     (".git/parallax/attestations/abc.json", 0),
 413:     (".git/parallax/application-checkpoints/abc.md", 0),
 414:     ("rounds/x", 1),
 415:     (".superpowers/review-sources/x", 1),
 416:     (".superpowers/sdd/plan/progress.md", 1),
 417:     ("docs/superpowers/rounds/x", 1),
 418: ])
 419: def test_assert_answers_membership_in_the_retained_set(tmp_path, rel, expect):
 420:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 421:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", str(repo / rel))
 422:     assert proc.returncode == expect, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 423:     assert "assert: " in proc.stdout
 424: 
 425: 
 426: @needs_host
 427: def test_assert_rejects_a_path_outside_the_repo(tmp_path):
 428:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 429:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert",
 430:                         str(tmp_path / "elsewhere" / "x"))
 431:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 432:     assert "outside every retained root" in proc.stdout
 433: 
 434: 
 435: @needs_host
 436: def test_assert_follows_the_override(tmp_path):
 437:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
 438:     (repo / "dev" / "docs" / "superpowers").mkdir(parents=True)
 439:     inside = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert",
 440:                           str(repo / "dev/docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/2026-09-12-x/r1.md"))
 441:     assert inside.returncode == 0, inside.stdout
 442:     stale = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert",
 443:                          str(repo / "docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/2026-09-12-x/r1.md"))
 444:     assert stale.returncode == 1, stale.stdout
 445: ```
 446: 
 447: - [ ] **Step 2: Run the resolver group and confirm it fails on the missing tool**
 448: 
 449: Run: `python -m pytest evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py -q -k "resolver or override or docsroot or reporoot or missing or assert"`
 450: Expected: every test FAILS (PowerShell reports the script path does not exist; returncode is not 0).
 451: 
 452: - [ ] **Step 3: Write the tool**
 453: 
 454: Create `tools/artifact-roots.ps1`:
 455: 
 456: ```powershell
 457: # artifact-roots.ps1 - resolve every path a multi-model-verify round writes
 458: # into a consumer repository, from the ONE declaration in
 459: # skills/multi-model-verify/references/model-prompting-notes.md.
 460: #
 461: # Item 100 (2026-09-12): rounds, ledgers and mirrors landed in three roots
 462: # per consumer repo because each writer read the repo-side override on
 463: # its own. This tool is the only reader. It prints the resolved set for
 464: # the debate record, and -Assert answers whether one path lies inside a
 465: # retained in-repo root before a retention copy runs.
 466: #
 467: # The declaration is PARSED here, never remembered: a missing or doubled
 468: # line is an error, not a default.
 469: #
 470: # Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, ASCII ONLY.
 471: #
 472: # Exit codes: 0 resolved (or -Assert inside), 1 -Assert outside,
 473: # 2 parameter fault, unreadable declaration, or -RepoRoot not a git
 474: # working tree. The map mirrors dispatch-round.ps1.
 475: param(
 476:     [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$RepoRoot,
 477:     [string]$DocsRoot = "",
 478:     [string]$Assert = "",
 479:     [switch]$Json
 480: )
 481: 
 482: $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
 483: 
 484: function Fail($message) {
 485:     Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $message)
 486:     exit 2
 487: }
 488: 
 489: function Normalize-Slashes($p) {
 490:     return $p.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
 491: }
 492: 
 493: function Resolve-Absolute($p) {
 494:     # Provider-relative, like new-review-mirror.ps1: a relative path
 495:     # resolves against PowerShell's location, not the process cwd.
 496:     $full = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($p)
 497:     return Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($full))
 498: }
 499: 
 500: # ---- the declaration ---------------------------------------------------
 501: $NotesPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\skills\multi-model-verify\references\model-prompting-notes.md"
 502: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $NotesPath -PathType Leaf)) {
 503:     Fail ("declaration file not found: " + $NotesPath)
 504: }
 505: $notes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($NotesPath, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
 506: $regionMatch = [regex]::Match($notes,
 507:     '<!-- contract:start id=artifact-roots -->(.*?)<!-- contract:end -->',
 508:     [System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions]::Singleline)
 509: if (-not $regionMatch.Success) {
 510:     Fail ("artifact-roots region not found in " + $NotesPath)
 511: }
 512: $region = $regionMatch.Groups[1].Value
 513: 
 514: $labels = @(
 515:     @{ Key = "docsRoot";         Label = "Canonical docs root" },
 516:     @{ Key = "docsRootOverride"; Label = "Canonical docs root override" },
 517:     @{ Key = "frozenPlan";       Label = "Canonical frozen plan path" },
 518:     @{ Key = "rounds";           Label = "Canonical rounds root" },
 519:     @{ Key = "sddLedger";        Label = "Canonical SDD ledger root" },
 520:     @{ Key = "reviewMirror";     Label = "Canonical review mirror root" },
 521:     @{ Key = "attestation";      Label = "Canonical attestation root" },
 522:     @{ Key = "checkpoint";       Label = "Canonical checkpoint root" }
 523: )
 524: $declared = @{}
 525: foreach ($l in $labels) {
 526:     $pattern = '(?m)^' + [regex]::Escape($l.Label) + ': `([^`\r\n]+)`[ \t]*\r?$'
 527:     $hits = [regex]::Matches($region, $pattern)
 528:     if ($hits.Count -ne 1) {
 529:         Fail ("declaration line '" + $l.Label + "' found " + $hits.Count +
 530:             " times in the artifact-roots region; expected exactly 1")
 531:     }
 532:     $declared[$l.Key] = $hits[0].Groups[1].Value
 533: }
 534: 
 535: # ---- the repository ----------------------------------------------------
 536: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $RepoRoot -PathType Container)) {
 537:     Fail ("-RepoRoot is not a directory: " + $RepoRoot)
 538: }
 539: # Native git under Continue: a benign stderr line must not become a
 540: # terminating error (CLAUDE.md, the dispatch traps).
 541: $priorEap = $ErrorActionPreference
 542: $ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
 543: $toplevel = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
 544: $topExit = $LASTEXITCODE
 545: $commonDir = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --git-common-dir 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
 546: $commonExit = $LASTEXITCODE
 547: $ErrorActionPreference = $priorEap
 548: if (($topExit -ne 0) -or -not $toplevel) {
 549:     Fail ("-RepoRoot is not a git working tree: " + $RepoRoot)
 550: }
 551: if (($commonExit -ne 0) -or -not $commonDir) {
 552:     Fail ("could not resolve the git common dir for " + $RepoRoot)
 553: }
 554: $top = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($toplevel))
 555: if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($commonDir)) {
 556:     $commonDir = Join-Path $toplevel $commonDir
 557: }
 558: $common = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($commonDir))
 559: 
 560: # ---- the docs root -----------------------------------------------------
 561: if ($PSBoundParameters.ContainsKey("DocsRoot")) {
 562:     $rel = $DocsRoot.Replace("\", "/").Trim("/")
 563:     if (-not $rel) { Fail "-DocsRoot is empty" }
 564:     if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($DocsRoot)) {
 565:         Fail ("-DocsRoot must be relative to the repo root: " + $DocsRoot)
 566:     }
 567:     if (@($rel.Split("/")) -contains "..") {
 568:         Fail ("-DocsRoot may not contain a '..' segment: " + $DocsRoot)
 569:     }
 570:     $docsRel = $rel
 571:     $source = "-DocsRoot"
 572: } elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $toplevel $declared.docsRootOverride) -PathType Container) {
 573:     $docsRel = $declared.docsRootOverride
 574:     $source = "override directory exists"
 575: } else {
 576:     $docsRel = $declared.docsRoot
 577:     $source = "default"
 578: }
 579: 
 580: $tempRoot = $env:TEMP
 581: if (-not $tempRoot) { $tempRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempPath() }
 582: $tempRoot = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($tempRoot))
 583: 
 584: function Resolve-Row($value) {
 585:     $v = $value.Replace("<docs-root>", $docsRel)
 586:     $v = $v.Replace("<TEMP>", $tempRoot)
 587:     $v = $v.Replace("<git-common-dir>", $common)
 588:     if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($v)) { return Normalize-Slashes $v }
 589:     return ($top + "/" + (Normalize-Slashes $v))
 590: }
 591: 
 592: $resolved = [ordered]@{
 593:     repo         = $top
 594:     docsRoot     = $top + "/" + $docsRel
 595:     source       = $source
 596:     frozenPlan   = Resolve-Row $declared.frozenPlan
 597:     rounds       = Resolve-Row $declared.rounds
 598:     sddLedger    = Resolve-Row $declared.sddLedger
 599:     reviewMirror = Resolve-Row $declared.reviewMirror
 600:     attestation  = Resolve-Row $declared.attestation
 601:     checkpoint   = Resolve-Row $declared.checkpoint
 602: }
 603: 
 604: # ---- -Assert -----------------------------------------------------------
 605: function Strip-Placeholder($p) {
 606:     # The retained root is the path up to the first per-debate
 607:     # placeholder: ".../plans/<date>-<topic>.md" asserts under ".../plans".
 608:     $i = $p.IndexOf("<")
 609:     $head = if ($i -ge 0) { $p.Substring(0, $i) } else { $p }
 610:     return $head.TrimEnd("/")
 611: }
 612: 
 613: $assertResult = $null
 614: $exitCode = 0
 615: if ($PSBoundParameters.ContainsKey("Assert")) {
 616:     if (-not $Assert) { Fail "-Assert is empty" }
 617:     $target = Resolve-Absolute $Assert
 618:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
 619:     # Rounds before the plan parent: the rounds root sits under it and
 620:     # the more specific name is the useful answer.
 621:     $retained = @(
 622:         @{ Name = "rounds root";        Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.rounds },
 623:         @{ Name = "frozen plan parent"; Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.frozenPlan },
 624:         @{ Name = "attestation root";   Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.attestation },
 625:         @{ Name = "checkpoint root";    Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.checkpoint }
 626:     )
 627:     $inside = $null
 628:     foreach ($r in $retained) {
 629:         if ($target.Equals($r.Root, $cmp) -or $target.StartsWith($r.Root + "/", $cmp)) {
 630:             $inside = $r.Name
 631:             break
 632:         }
 633:     }
 634:     if ($inside) {
 635:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $true; root = $inside }
 636:         $exitCode = 0
 637:     } else {
 638:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $false; root = "" }
 639:         $exitCode = 1
 640:     }
 641: }
 642: 
 643: # ---- output ------------------------------------------------------------
 644: if ($Json) {
 645:     $out = [ordered]@{}
 646:     foreach ($k in $resolved.Keys) { $out[$k] = $resolved[$k] }
 647:     if ($assertResult) { $out["assert"] = $assertResult }
 648:     Write-Output (ConvertTo-Json $out -Depth 3)
 649: } else {
 650:     Write-Output ("repo: " + $resolved.repo)
 651:     Write-Output ("docs-root: " + $resolved.docsRoot)
 652:     Write-Output ("docs-root source: " + $resolved.source)
 653:     Write-Output ("frozen-plan: " + $resolved.frozenPlan)
 654:     Write-Output ("rounds: " + $resolved.rounds)
 655:     Write-Output ("sdd-ledger: " + $resolved.sddLedger)
 656:     Write-Output ("review-mirror: " + $resolved.reviewMirror)
 657:     Write-Output ("attestation: " + $resolved.attestation)
 658:     Write-Output ("checkpoint: " + $resolved.checkpoint)
 659:     if ($assertResult) {
 660:         if ($assertResult.inside) {
 661:             Write-Output ("assert: inside " + $assertResult.root + ": " + $assertResult.path)
 662:         } else {
 663:             Write-Output ("assert: outside every retained root: " + $assertResult.path)
 664:         }
 665:     }
 666: }
 667: exit $exitCode
 668: ```

 705: - [ ] **Step 1: Append the sweep test**

 707: Append to `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py`:
 708: 
 709: ```python
 710: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 711: # Group 2: the static sweep
 712: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 713: PLUGIN_SURFACE = ("skills/**/*.md", "agents/*.md", "commands/*.md",
 714:                   "hooks/*", "tools/*.ps1")
 715: 
 716: # A declaration line in the notes is the one place a root may be spelled.
 717: DECLARATION_LINE = re.compile(r"^Canonical [a-zA-Z ]+: `[^`]+`\s*$")
 718: 
 719: # The shapes are ENUMERATED so the failure names what was searched for.
 720: # A dated citation under the declared rounds root
 721: # (docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-...) matches none of them; a
 722: # dated ledger citation (.superpowers/sdd/<date>-...) is exempted by the
 723: # lookahead, because one exists in the notes today.
 724: FORBIDDEN_SHAPES = [
 725:     ("a rounds root beside plans/ instead of under it",
 726:      re.compile(r"(?<!plans/)superpowers/rounds/")),
 727:     ("the foreign controller's mirror root",
 728:      re.compile(r"review-sources")),
 729:     ("the override docs root named by hand",
 730:      re.compile(r"dev/docs/superpowers")),
 731:     ("a ledger root that is neither the declaration nor a dated citation",
 732:      re.compile(r"\.superpowers/sdd/(?!\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-)")),
 733: ]
 734: 
 735: 
 736: def test_no_round_root_is_named_outside_the_declaration():
 737:     offenders = []
 738:     for pattern in PLUGIN_SURFACE:
 739:         for f in sorted(REPO.glob(pattern)):
 740:             if not f.is_file():
 741:                 continue
 742:             for lineno, line in enumerate(read(f).splitlines(), 1):
 743:                 if f == NOTES and DECLARATION_LINE.match(line):
 744:                     continue
 745:                 for label, rx in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES:
 746:                     if rx.search(line):
 747:                         offenders.append(
 748:                             f"{f.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()}:{lineno}: {label}")
 749:     searched = "; ".join(label for label, _ in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES)
 750:     assert not offenders, (
 751:         "a round root is named outside the declaration (searched for: "
 752:         + searched + "):\n" + "\n".join(offenders))
 753: 
 754: 
 755: def test_sweep_can_fail(tmp_path):
 756:     # The negative control: the same shapes against a line each of the
 757:     # two KitnEssentials roots would produce.
 758:     hits = [label for label, rx in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES
 759:             if rx.search("see dev/docs/superpowers/rounds/2026-09-02-x/")]
 760:     assert "a rounds root beside plans/ instead of under it" in hits
 761:     assert "the override docs root named by hand" in hits
 762:     hits = [label for label, rx in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES
 763:             if rx.search(".superpowers/review-sources/dt-diag-2766cd59/")]
 764:     assert hits == ["the foreign controller's mirror root"]
 765:     assert not [label for label, rx in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES
 766:                 if rx.search("docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/2026-08-03-x/")]
 767:     assert not [label for label, rx in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES
 768:                 if rx.search(".superpowers/sdd/2026-08-15-x/progress.md")]
 769: ```

 978: - [ ] **Step 3: Append the writer tests**
 979: 
 980: Append to `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py`:
 981: 
 982: ```python
 983: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 984: # Group 3b: the real writers
 985: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 986: def tree_paths(root):
 987:     """Every FILE under root as a repo-relative normalized path, .git
 988:     included. A SET OF PATHS, not contents: the mirror tool's status
 989:     capture rewrites .git/index in place (new-review-mirror.ps1, the
 990:     status capture), so a content diff would fire on a correct tool and
 991:     a path-set diff does not."""
 992:     return {norm(p.relative_to(root)) for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
 993: 
 994: 
 995: def new_paths(root, before):
 996:     return tree_paths(root) - before
 997: 
 998: 
 999: def write_attestation(repo, base, head):
1000:     return subprocess.run(
1001:         [POWERSHELL, "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", str(ATTEST),
1002:          "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
1003:          "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
1004:          "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
1005:          "-Participants", "t (session) / t (reviewer)"],
1006:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
1007: 
1008: 
1009: @needs_host
1010: def test_the_real_writers_create_nothing_in_repo_but_the_attestation(tmp_path):
1011:     # The three tools that write during a round, run for real against a
1012:     # disposable two-commit repository. The only path that may appear
1013:     # inside the repository is the attestation, and it must satisfy the
1014:     # resolver's own membership answer.
1015:     from test_dispatch_round import build_real_mirror, prepare_default
1016:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path, name="src", commits=2)
1017:     base = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD~1").strip()
1018:     head = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").strip()
1019:     before = tree_paths(repo)
1020: 
1021:     att = write_attestation(repo, base, head)
1022:     assert att.returncode == 0, att.stdout + att.stderr
1023:     mirror = build_real_mirror(tmp_path, source=repo)
1024:     assert norm(mirror.source) == norm(repo)
1025:     prep = prepare_default(tmp_path, mirror=mirror)
1026:     assert prep.returncode == 0, prep.stdout + prep.stderr
1027: 
1028:     appeared = new_paths(repo, before)
1029:     assert appeared == {f".git/parallax/attestations/{head}.json"}, sorted(appeared)
1030:     for rel in appeared:
1031:         proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", str(repo / rel))
1032:         assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1033:         assert "assert: inside attestation root" in proc.stdout
1034: 
1035: 
1036: @needs_host
1037: def test_a_writer_that_strays_is_reported(tmp_path):
1038:     # Negative control for the diff-and-assert logic above: a stub writer
1039:     # that lands a round record beside the plans root is caught by the
1040:     # same path-set diff and refused by the same membership answer.
1041:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1042:     before = tree_paths(repo)
1043:     stray = repo / "rounds" / "x"
1044:     stray.parent.mkdir()
1045:     stray.write_text("a round record in the wrong root\n")
1046:     appeared = new_paths(repo, before)
1047:     assert appeared == {"rounds/x"}, sorted(appeared)
1048:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", str(stray))
1049:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1050:     assert "outside every retained root" in proc.stdout
1051: 
1052: 
1053: @needs_host
1054: def test_the_mirror_row_is_enforced_by_the_mirror_tool(tmp_path):
1055:     # The declaration's `Canonical review mirror root` is fixed outside
1056:     # the repository because the tool refuses anything else. The refusal
1057:     # is pinned in test_review_mirror.py; this one cites the row.
1058:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1059:     proc = subprocess.run(
1060:         [POWERSHELL, "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", str(MIRROR_TOOL),
1061:          "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-MirrorPath", str(repo / "inside" / "mirror"),
1062:          "-SkipProbe"],
1063:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120)
1064:     assert proc.returncode == 2, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1065:     assert "inside the repo" in proc.stdout
1066: ```

 exited 1 in 965ms:
   1: # Design: one declaration of every path a round writes
   2: 
   3: **Filed 2026-09-12** for BACKLOG item 100. A KitnEssentials cleanup on
   4: this date found parallax round artifacts across four roots in one
   5: repository, written by different plugin versions and by writers that
   6: honoured the repo's `docs/superpowers -> dev/docs/superpowers` override
   7: inconsistently. KitnEssentials archives its own history by hand; this
   8: design makes the plugin stop adding to the spread.
   9: 
  10: ## The measured facts
  11: 
  12: 1. The plugin has no single statement of where a debate writes. The
  13:    frozen plan and the rounds root are named in
  14:    `skills/multi-model-verify/references/frozen-plan-format.md:27` and
  15:    `:85`, with the KitnEssentials override written as an inline example
  16:    at `:28` and again at `skills/multi-model-verify/SKILL.md:323`.
  17:    The attestation root is named in `SKILL.md:389` and computed in
  18:    `tools/write-attestation.ps1:72` and `tools/verify-attestation.ps1:177`;
  19:    the checkpoint root is computed in `tools/write-attestation.ps1:99`
  20:    and `tools/verify-attestation.ps1:125` and named in
  21:    `references/application-checkpoint.md:80`. The mirror root is prose in
  22:    `references/preflight-mirror.md:12` ("a SHORT `<scratch>` directly
  23:    under the temp directory"). The SDD ledger root is not named by the
  24:    plugin at all; `agents/fable-reviewer.md:18` takes it as an argument.
  25: 2. The SDD ledger root is not the plugin's to place. Superpowers
  26:    `subagent-driven-development/scripts/sdd-workspace:35-39` hard-codes
  27:    `<repo-root>/.superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>/` and writes a
  28:    self-ignoring `.gitignore` beside it, and that skill's `SKILL.md:141`
  29:    reads the ledger back from `<workspace>/progress.md`, so a plugin that
  30:    moved the ledger would break the skill it is built on.
  31: 3. The review mirror must sit outside the reviewed repository.
  32:    `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1460` already refuses a `-MirrorPath`
  33:    equal to, inside, or containing the repo, and
  34:    `evals/multi-model-verify/test_review_mirror.py:565` pins the refusal.
  35: 4. Two of the four KitnEssentials roots were not written by any parallax
  36:    writer. `dev/docs/superpowers/rounds/` has no source in this repo's
  37:    history at any version (`git log -S'superpowers/rounds' --all` finds
  38:    only the backlog filing). The 54 MB
  39:    `.superpowers/review-sources/dt-diag-2766cd59/` was written on
  40:    2026-09-07 by a Codex controller session that invented a "preparation
  41:    copy" of a worktree; its rollout log says so in its own words. The
  42:    plugin binds its own tools and the prose the Claude controller
  43:    follows. It cannot bind a foreign controller, and this design does
  44:    not claim to.
  45: 5. The repo-side override has no mechanical form. KitnEssentials
  46:    declares it as a prose line in its `CLAUDE.md` ("wherever a plugin
  47:    skill says `docs/superpowers/...`, read it as
  48:    `dev/docs/superpowers/...`"). A tool cannot read that reliably, and a
  49:    session reading it per writer is the drift the item describes.
  50: 
  51: ## Decisions taken in brainstorming (2026-09-12)
  52: 
  53: - **Override discovery is existence-based, with an explicit escape.**
  54:   The docs root is `dev/docs/superpowers` when that directory exists
  55:   under the repo root, else `docs/superpowers`; a `-DocsRoot` argument
  56:   overrides both, and the printed line names which rule fired. No
  57:   consumer setup is needed and KitnEssentials resolves correctly today.
  58: - **The ledger and mirror roots are declared FIXED, with their reasons,
  59:   and item 100's closing criteria are edited in place to match.** The
  60:   backlog asked for the mirror and ledger roots to be gitignored by the
  61:   same entry as the rounds. Facts 2 and 3 make that impossible without
  62:   forking the vendor script or moving the mirror into the repo, and
  63:   neither trade is worth the single gitignore line.
  64: 
  65: ## The declaration
  66: 
  67: A new section in
  68: `skills/multi-model-verify/references/model-prompting-notes.md`, placed
  69: AFTER the backup reviewer lane block (the primary model declarations
  70: must stay first, because two runtime parsers match the first
  71: `Canonical model id:` occurrence). It is wrapped in
  72: `<!-- contract:start id=artifact-roots -->` / `contract:end` so the
  73: coverage checker in `test_contract_coverage.py` requires a pin, and it
  74: is registered in that file's `DECLARED_REGIONS`. The region holds
  75: EXACTLY the eight declaration lines below and nothing else, and ONE pin
  76: in `test_artifact_roots.py` holds the whole region text: the checker
  77: folds a region into one body and a pin that stops mid-region or two
  78: pins that jointly span it leave it unlocked
  79: (`test_contract_coverage.py:537-553`). The explanatory prose sits
  80: outside the markers.
  81: 
  82: The machine-read lines, in the same one-value-per-line style as the
  83: model declarations:
  84: 
  85: ```
  86: Canonical docs root: `docs/superpowers`
  87: Canonical docs root override: `dev/docs/superpowers`
  88: Canonical frozen plan path: `<docs-root>/plans/<date>-<topic>.md`
  89: Canonical rounds root: `<docs-root>/plans/rounds/<date>-<topic>/`
  90: Canonical SDD ledger root: `.superpowers/sdd/<plan-basename>/`
  91: Canonical review mirror root: `<TEMP>/<short-name>/`
  92: Canonical attestation root: `<git-common-dir>/parallax/attestations/`
  93: Canonical checkpoint root: `<git-common-dir>/parallax/application-checkpoints/`
  94: ```
  95: 
  96: `<git-common-dir>` is what `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` returns for
  97: the repo, which is what `tools/write-attestation.ps1:54-63` and
  98: `tools/verify-attestation.ps1:125` already resolve, and what
  99: `references/application-checkpoint.md:80` already writes. In a linked
 100: worktree `.git` is a file and the common dir is elsewhere, so a row
 101: spelled `.git/parallax/...` would name a path the emitter never writes.
 102: 
 103: The prose around them states, for each fixed row, why it is fixed:
 104: 
 105: - SDD ledger: Superpowers-owned; its `sdd-workspace` script writes the
 106:   directory and the self-ignoring `.gitignore`; the plugin cites it and
 107:   never relocates it.
 108: - Review mirror: never inside the reviewed repository; the tool refuses
 109:   an in-repo path; `<TEMP>` is `$env:TEMP` on the controller host.
 110: - Attestation and checkpoint: under the repo's git common dir so that
 111:   recording a verdict cannot move `HEAD`; the verifier re-hashes the
 112:   checkpoint there.
 113: 
 114: And two rules that are prose because no tool can enforce them:
 115: 
 116: - Dispatch directories, receipts, briefs, prior-state files and the
 117:   probe's override file live in session scratch OUTSIDE the repository
 118:   for the whole round (the quiet period in
 119:   `references/preflight-mirror.md` forbids in-repo writes anyway). Only
 120:   their retained COPIES enter the rounds root, and only after the
 121:   wrapper exits.
 122: - A controller other than Claude Code is outside this contract. Fact 4
 123:   is the record of what one wrote.
 124: 
 125: The two overridable rows are exactly the ones containing `<docs-root>`.
 126: The resolver finds them by that placeholder, so adding an overridable
 127: row later is one declaration line, not a tool edit.
 128: 
 129: ## The resolver
 130: 
 131: `tools/artifact-roots.ps1`, new, following the conventions the other
 132: tools already carry (both PowerShell hosts, literal paths, no `Stop`
 133: preference around native calls, `-Json` output shape).
 134: 
 135: ```
 136: -RepoRoot <path>            required; must be a git working tree
 137: -DocsRoot <relative path>   optional; explicit override, relative to the
 138:                             repo root, no `..` segment, no rooted path
 139: -Assert <path>              optional; exit 0 when the path (existing or
 140:                             not) lies inside one of the retained in-repo
 141:                             roots, 1 otherwise
 142: -Json                       optional
 143: ```
 144: 
 145: Behaviour:
 146: 
 147: 1. Read the declaration from the plugin's own
 148:    `references/model-prompting-notes.md`, located relative to
 149:    `$PSScriptRoot`. Every one of the eight `Canonical ... :` lines must
 150:    be present inside the `artifact-roots` region; a missing line, a
 151:    missing region, or a duplicate line is an ERROR with exit 2. A
 152:    remembered path is never substituted.
 153: 2. Resolve `<docs-root>`: `-DocsRoot` if given; else the override value
 154:    if `<RepoRoot>/<override>` exists as a directory; else the default.
 155:    Record the source as one of `-DocsRoot`, `override directory
 156:    exists`, `default`.
 157: 3. Substitute `<docs-root>` in the two overridable rows, `<TEMP>` in
 158:    the mirror row, and `<git-common-dir>` in the attestation and
 159:    checkpoint rows from `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` run in
 160:    `-RepoRoot`. Leave `<date>-<topic>`, `<plan-basename>` and
 161:    `<short-name>` as printed placeholders: they are named per debate.
 162: 4. Print one line per row, `name: <absolute path>`, then
 163:    `docs-root source: <source>`. With `-Json`, emit one object with the
 164:    same keys plus `source`. The absolute path for a placeholder-bearing
 165:    row is the row's PARENT resolved plus the placeholder tail, e.g.
 166:    `C:/repo/dev/docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-<topic>/`.
 167: 5. `-Assert`: canonicalize the path (full path, forward slashes, no
 168:    trailing separator) and test membership under the frozen-plan
 169:    parent, the rounds root, the attestation root and the checkpoint
 170:    root, each with its placeholder tail removed. Exit 0 on membership,
 171:    1 otherwise, printing which root matched or `outside every retained
 172:    root`. The mirror and ledger roots are NOT in the `-Assert` set: the
 173:    session never copies into them.
 174: 
 175: Exit map: 0 resolved (or asserted inside), 1 asserted outside, 2 for a
 176: parameter fault, an unreadable declaration, or a `-RepoRoot` that is not
 177: a git working tree. The map mirrors `tools/dispatch-round.ps1`'s so a
 178: caller reads one convention.
 179: 
 180: ## Skill and agent edits
 181: 
 182: - `SKILL.md` preflight gains step 4, both modes, as ONE line: run
 183:   `tools/artifact-roots.ps1 -RepoRoot <repo>` and follow the operating
 184:   rule in `references/model-prompting-notes.md`. That rule, written in
 185:   prose beside the declaration, is: the resolver's output goes to
 186:   session scratch OUTSIDE the repo (it is then a retained artifact like
 187:   a brief, and enters the rounds root as `artifact-roots.txt` only after
 188:   the wrapper exits, so the quiet period is untouched and no ordering
 189:   rule against the mirror build is needed); every later act that names
 190:   a path uses the printed one, the frozen plan save, the rounds
 191:   retention, the ledger citation in the fable-reviewer dispatch, the
 192:   attestation emitter's expected output; and the retention copy runs
 193:   `-Assert` on its destination first.
 194: - `SKILL.md` is at 6496 of `skill_lint.py`'s 6500-token hard ceiling
 195:   (measured 2026-09-12 with `--strict`; `BACKLOG.md:4219-4227` records
 196:   the same). The one-line step is paid for by mode plan step 5
 197:   (`SKILL.md:321-324`), whose parenthetical naming the KitnEssentials
 198:   and default plans directories is removed and replaced by "at the
 199:   frozen-plan path the preflight printed". The plan's task runs the
 200:   linter after the edit; if the file is still over the ceiling the task
 201:   STOPS and asks the user what to remove, because that choice is the
 202:   user's (the backlog entry says so), not the implementer's.
 203: - `frozen-plan-format.md:27-28` and `:85` stop naming the KitnEssentials
 204:   path by hand and cite the declaration's frozen-plan and rounds rows.
 205: - `references/preflight-mirror.md` and `references/backup-lane.md` cite
 206:   the mirror row where they describe the scratch location; the
 207:   operational text (short name, path budget) stays where it is.
 208: - `agents/fable-reviewer.md:18` cites the ledger row so the dispatcher
 209:   knows where the path it is handing over comes from.
 210: - `commands/doctor.md` is NOT extended. The resolver is a per-debate
 211:   preflight, not a health probe.
 212: 
 213: No pin-bearing sentence is reflowed; the edits are additions and
 214: citation swaps, checked against `test_contract_coverage.py` and the raw
 215: pins in `test_multi_model_verify.py` before the wording is settled.
 216: 
 217: ## The eval
 218: 
 219: `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py`, new, added to BOTH
 220: host steps of the `powershell-hosts` job AND to
 221: `REQUIRED_DUAL_HOST_MODULES` in `evals/tools/check_workflow_paths.py:61`
 222: in the same act: that list is what locks a module into both steps, and
 223: a module named in the workflow but absent from the list is not locked
 224: (the comment at `:64-67` says so, and `test_dispatch_round.py` is an
 225: existing example of the gap).
 226: 
 227: Three groups:
 228: 
 229: 1. **Declaration.** The region exists, the primary `Canonical model id:`
 230:    still precedes it, and one pin in the `"literal" in body` form holds
 231:    the region's whole text, all eight lines as one folded literal.
 232: 2. **Static consistency sweep**, the same shape as the no-hardcoded-`-m`
 233:    test. Every path literal in `skills/`, `agents/`, `commands/`,
 234:    `hooks/` and `tools/` that names a round-artifact root must be one
 235:    the declaration states. The forbidden shapes are enumerated so the
 236:    sweep reports what it searched for: `superpowers/rounds/` not preceded
 237:    by `plans/`, `review-sources`, `dev/docs/superpowers` anywhere but
 238:    the declaration's override line, and `.superpowers/sdd/` anywhere but
 239:    the declaration's ledger line or a dated citation. A dated citation
 240:    is a path under `docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-` or
 241:    `.superpowers/sdd/<date>-` naming a retained record (the plugin
 242:    surface carries one of the latter today, at
 243:    `references/model-prompting-notes.md:87`). Measured 2026-09-12 by the
 244:    Fable pre-read: the first two shapes have zero hits in the plugin
 245:    surface, all seven `superpowers/plans/rounds/` hits are preceded by
 246:    `plans/`, and the two `dev/docs/superpowers` hits are the ones this
 247:    design removes. A bare `rounds/` literal has no static shape, because
 248:    the word is too common to sweep; `-Assert` is what covers it.
 249: 3. **Behavioural, real tools, both hosts.** The module carries its own
 250:    module-level skip when no PowerShell host is on PATH; the mark in
 251:    `test_dispatch_round.py:32-35` does not travel with an import. The
 252:    test builds a disposable source repository with TWO commits, because
 253:    `write-attestation.ps1:67-70` refuses base and head that resolve to
 254:    one commit, and `build_real_mirror` in `test_dispatch_round.py:173`
 255:    creates its own one-commit source with no way to pass one in. So the
 256:    plan extends that fixture with an optional `source` argument whose
 257:    default keeps today's behaviour, and every existing caller is
 258:    unchanged.
 259:    - the resolver prints the default set; after `mkdir
 260:      dev/docs/superpowers`, it prints the override set with source
 261:      `override directory exists`; with `-DocsRoot other/root` it prints
 262:      that with source `-DocsRoot`; `-DocsRoot ../x` and a rooted value
 263:      exit 2; a `-RepoRoot` that is not a git tree exits 2.
 264:    - `-Assert` exits 0 for a path under the resolved rounds root and the
 265:      attestation root, 1 for `<repo>/rounds/x`, `<repo>/.superpowers/
 266:      review-sources/x`, and a path outside the repo.
 267:    - the writers: snapshot the repo tree as a SET OF PATHS (every path,
 268:      ignored ones included; `.git/index` is rewritten by the status
 269:      capture in `new-review-mirror.ps1:1665-1669`, so a content diff
 270:      would fire on a correct tool and a path-set diff does not), run
 271:      `tools/write-attestation.ps1` for the two commits, run
 272:      `tools/dispatch-round.ps1 -Prepare` through the extended
 273:      `build_real_mirror` and `prepare_default` with the dispatch
 274:      directory and receipt outside the repo, and run
 275:      `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1` to a temp path with `-SkipProbe`;
 276:      diff the sets; assert every path that APPEARED inside the repo
 277:      satisfies `-Assert`, and that the set is exactly the attestation
 278:      file.
 279:    - negative control, so the diff logic is shown able to fail: a stub
 280:      writer creates `<repo>/rounds/x`, and the same diff-and-assert
 281:      reports it.
 282:    - the mirror tool still refuses an in-repo `-MirrorPath` (the
 283:      existing pin stays; this test cites the declaration row in its
 284:      name).
 285: 
 286: What group 3 proves is bounded: only a tool that CREATES a path is
 287: caught, only the three tools are run, and only in the modes the test
 288: exercises (`-SkipProbe`, and whatever `prepare_default` passes). A tool
 289: that rewrites an existing in-repo file is outside it.
 290: 
 291: The behavioural group imports the fixtures from `test_dispatch_round.py`
 292: as a sibling module; pytest's default import mode puts the test
 293: directory on `sys.path`, which is how `test_contract_coverage.py:12`
 294: reaches `contract_coverage.py` with no `conftest.py` or package marker
 295: under `evals/`.
 296: 
 297: ## What the eval does not prove
 298: 
 299: The rounds retention is a session act, a copy from scratch into the
 300: rounds root at the end of a debate. The eval proves the resolver names
 301: the right destination and the skill text sends the session there; it
 302: cannot prove a session obeyed. The `-Assert` step before the copy is the
 303: mechanical half of that act, and the behavioural runner
 304: (`evals/tools/run_behavioral_evals.py`) is where obedience is measured.
 305: 
 306: ## Backlog
 307: 
 308: Item 100's "What closing it means" paragraph is edited in place, dated
 309: 2026-09-12, to state the ledger and mirror roots as fixed with their
 310: reasons and to drop the shared-gitignore sentence. Status stays OPEN
 311: until the diff debate closes it.
 312: 
 313: ## Out of scope
 314: 
 315: - Migrating any consumer tree. KitnEssentials archives by hand.
 316: - The plugin's own `tools/drift-reports/` and `drift-snapshot.json`.
 317:   They are plugin-side state in the plugin checkout, not consumer-repo

452: - [ ] **Step 3: Write the tool**
453: 
454: Create `tools/artifact-roots.ps1`:
455: 
456: ```powershell
457: # artifact-roots.ps1 - resolve every path a multi-model-verify round writes
458: # into a consumer repository, from the ONE declaration in
459: # skills/multi-model-verify/references/model-prompting-notes.md.
460: #
461: # Item 100 (2026-09-12): rounds, ledgers and mirrors landed in three roots
462: # per consumer repo because each writer read the repo-side override on
463: # its own. This tool is the only reader. It prints the resolved set for
464: # the debate record, and -Assert answers whether one path lies inside a
465: # retained in-repo root before a retention copy runs.
466: #
467: # The declaration is PARSED here, never remembered: a missing or doubled
468: # line is an error, not a default.
469: #
470: # Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, ASCII ONLY.
471: #
472: # Exit codes: 0 resolved (or -Assert inside), 1 -Assert outside,
473: # 2 parameter fault, unreadable declaration, or -RepoRoot not a git
474: # working tree. The map mirrors dispatch-round.ps1.
475: param(
476:     [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$RepoRoot,
477:     [string]$DocsRoot = "",
478:     [string]$Assert = "",
479:     [switch]$Json
480: )
481: 
482: $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
483: 
484: function Fail($message) {
485:     Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $message)
486:     exit 2
487: }
488: 
489: function Normalize-Slashes($p) {
490:     return $p.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/")
491: }
492: 
493: function Resolve-Absolute($p) {
494:     # Provider-relative, like new-review-mirror.ps1: a relative path
495:     # resolves against PowerShell's location, not the process cwd.
496:     $full = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($p)
497:     return Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($full))
498: }
499: 
500: # ---- the declaration ---------------------------------------------------
501: $NotesPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\skills\multi-model-verify\references\model-prompting-notes.md"
502: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $NotesPath -PathType Leaf)) {
503:     Fail ("declaration file not found: " + $NotesPath)
504: }
505: $notes = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($NotesPath, (New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
506: $regionMatch = [regex]::Match($notes,
507:     '<!-- contract:start id=artifact-roots -->(.*?)<!-- contract:end -->',
508:     [System.Text.RegularExpressions.RegexOptions]::Singleline)
509: if (-not $regionMatch.Success) {
510:     Fail ("artifact-roots region not found in " + $NotesPath)
511: }
512: $region = $regionMatch.Groups[1].Value
513: 
514: $labels = @(
515:     @{ Key = "docsRoot";         Label = "Canonical docs root" },
516:     @{ Key = "docsRootOverride"; Label = "Canonical docs root override" },
517:     @{ Key = "frozenPlan";       Label = "Canonical frozen plan path" },
518:     @{ Key = "rounds";           Label = "Canonical rounds root" },
519:     @{ Key = "sddLedger";        Label = "Canonical SDD ledger root" },
520:     @{ Key = "reviewMirror";     Label = "Canonical review mirror root" },
521:     @{ Key = "attestation";      Label = "Canonical attestation root" },
522:     @{ Key = "checkpoint";       Label = "Canonical checkpoint root" }
523: )
524: $declared = @{}
525: foreach ($l in $labels) {
526:     $pattern = '(?m)^' + [regex]::Escape($l.Label) + ': `([^`\r\n]+)`[ \t]*\r?$'
527:     $hits = [regex]::Matches($region, $pattern)
528:     if ($hits.Count -ne 1) {
529:         Fail ("declaration line '" + $l.Label + "' found " + $hits.Count +
530:             " times in the artifact-roots region; expected exactly 1")
531:     }
532:     $declared[$l.Key] = $hits[0].Groups[1].Value
533: }
534: 
535: # ---- the repository ----------------------------------------------------
536: if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $RepoRoot -PathType Container)) {
537:     Fail ("-RepoRoot is not a directory: " + $RepoRoot)
538: }
539: # Native git under Continue: a benign stderr line must not become a
540: # terminating error (CLAUDE.md, the dispatch traps).
541: $priorEap = $ErrorActionPreference
542: $ErrorActionPreference = "Continue"
543: $toplevel = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
544: $topExit = $LASTEXITCODE
545: $commonDir = (& git -C $RepoRoot rev-parse --git-common-dir 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
546: $commonExit = $LASTEXITCODE
547: $ErrorActionPreference = $priorEap
548: if (($topExit -ne 0) -or -not $toplevel) {
549:     Fail ("-RepoRoot is not a git working tree: " + $RepoRoot)
550: }
551: if (($commonExit -ne 0) -or -not $commonDir) {
552:     Fail ("could not resolve the git common dir for " + $RepoRoot)
553: }
554: $top = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($toplevel))
555: if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($commonDir)) {
556:     $commonDir = Join-Path $toplevel $commonDir
557: }
558: $common = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($commonDir))
559: 
560: # ---- the docs root -----------------------------------------------------
561: if ($PSBoundParameters.ContainsKey("DocsRoot")) {
562:     $rel = $DocsRoot.Replace("\", "/").Trim("/")
563:     if (-not $rel) { Fail "-DocsRoot is empty" }
564:     if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($DocsRoot)) {
565:         Fail ("-DocsRoot must be relative to the repo root: " + $DocsRoot)
566:     }
567:     if (@($rel.Split("/")) -contains "..") {
568:         Fail ("-DocsRoot may not contain a '..' segment: " + $DocsRoot)
569:     }
570:     $docsRel = $rel
571:     $source = "-DocsRoot"
572: } elseif (Test-Path -LiteralPath (Join-Path $toplevel $declared.docsRootOverride) -PathType Container) {
573:     $docsRel = $declared.docsRootOverride
574:     $source = "override directory exists"
575: } else {
576:     $docsRel = $declared.docsRoot
577:     $source = "default"
578: }
579: 
580: $tempRoot = $env:TEMP
581: if (-not $tempRoot) { $tempRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempPath() }
582: $tempRoot = Normalize-Slashes ([System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($tempRoot))
583: 
584: function Resolve-Row($value) {
585:     $v = $value.Replace("<docs-root>", $docsRel)
586:     $v = $v.Replace("<TEMP>", $tempRoot)
587:     $v = $v.Replace("<git-common-dir>", $common)
588:     if ([System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($v)) { return Normalize-Slashes $v }
589:     return ($top + "/" + (Normalize-Slashes $v))
590: }
591: 
592: $resolved = [ordered]@{
593:     repo         = $top
594:     docsRoot     = $top + "/" + $docsRel
595:     source       = $source
596:     frozenPlan   = Resolve-Row $declared.frozenPlan
597:     rounds       = Resolve-Row $declared.rounds
598:     sddLedger    = Resolve-Row $declared.sddLedger
599:     reviewMirror = Resolve-Row $declared.reviewMirror
600:     attestation  = Resolve-Row $declared.attestation
601:     checkpoint   = Resolve-Row $declared.checkpoint
602: }
603: 
604: # ---- -Assert -----------------------------------------------------------
605: function Strip-Placeholder($p) {
606:     # The retained root is the path up to the first per-debate
607:     # placeholder: ".../plans/<date>-<topic>.md" asserts under ".../plans".
608:     $i = $p.IndexOf("<")
609:     $head = if ($i -ge 0) { $p.Substring(0, $i) } else { $p }
610:     return $head.TrimEnd("/")
611: }
612: 
613: $assertResult = $null
614: $exitCode = 0
615: if ($PSBoundParameters.ContainsKey("Assert")) {
616:     if (-not $Assert) { Fail "-Assert is empty" }
617:     $target = Resolve-Absolute $Assert
618:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
619:     # Rounds before the plan parent: the rounds root sits under it and
620:     # the more specific name is the useful answer.
621:     $retained = @(
622:         @{ Name = "rounds root";        Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.rounds },
623:         @{ Name = "frozen plan parent"; Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.frozenPlan },
624:         @{ Name = "attestation root";   Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.attestation },
625:         @{ Name = "checkpoint root";    Root = Strip-Placeholder $resolved.checkpoint }
626:     )
627:     $inside = $null
628:     foreach ($r in $retained) {
629:         if ($target.Equals($r.Root, $cmp) -or $target.StartsWith($r.Root + "/", $cmp)) {
630:             $inside = $r.Name
631:             break
632:         }
633:     }
634:     if ($inside) {
635:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $true; root = $inside }
636:         $exitCode = 0
637:     } else {
638:         $assertResult = [ordered]@{ path = $target; inside = $false; root = "" }
639:         $exitCode = 1
640:     }
641: }
642: 
643: # ---- output ------------------------------------------------------------
644: if ($Json) {
645:     $out = [ordered]@{}
646:     foreach ($k in $resolved.Keys) { $out[$k] = $resolved[$k] }
647:     if ($assertResult) { $out["assert"] = $assertResult }
648:     Write-Output (ConvertTo-Json $out -Depth 3)
649: } else {
650:     Write-Output ("repo: " + $resolved.repo)

hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse Completed
hook: PreToolUse
hook: PreToolUse Completed
exec

 exited 1 in 794ms:

C:\Temp\pxar1\docs\superpowers\plans\rounds\2026-09-12-artifact-roots\brief-fable-spec-r1.md

702: - Consumes: the declaration labels from Task 1 and `tools/artifact-roots.ps1` from Task 2.
703: - Produces: SKILL.md preflight step 4 and the citation form `references/model-prompting-notes.md's artifact-roots declaration` used by every edited reference.

705: - [ ] **Step 1: Append the sweep test**
706: 
707: Append to `evals/multi-model-verify/test_artifact_roots.py`:
708: 
709: ```python
710: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
711: # Group 2: the static sweep
712: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
713: PLUGIN_SURFACE = ("skills/**/*.md", "agents/*.md", "commands/*.md",
714:                   "hooks/*", "tools/*.ps1")
715: 
716: # A declaration line in the notes is the one place a root may be spelled.
717: DECLARATION_LINE = re.compile(r"^Canonical [a-zA-Z ]+: `[^`]+`\s*$")
718: 
719: # The shapes are ENUMERATED so the failure names what was searched for.
720: # A dated citation under the declared rounds root
721: # (docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-...) matches none of them; a
722: # dated ledger citation (.superpowers/sdd/<date>-...) is exempted by the
723: # lookahead, because one exists in the notes today.
724: FORBIDDEN_SHAPES = [
725:     ("a rounds root beside plans/ instead of under it",
726:      re.compile(r"(?<!plans/)superpowers/rounds/")),
727:     ("the foreign controller's mirror root",
728:      re.compile(r"review-sources")),
729:     ("the override docs root named by hand",
730:      re.compile(r"dev/docs/superpowers")),
731:     ("a ledger root that is neither the declaration nor a dated citation",
732:      re.compile(r"\.superpowers/sdd/(?!\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-)")),
733: ]
734: 
735: 
736: def test_no_round_root_is_named_outside_the_declaration():
737:     offenders = []
738:     for pattern in PLUGIN_SURFACE:
739:         for f in sorted(REPO.glob(pattern)):
740:             if not f.is_file():
741:                 continue
742:             for lineno, line in enumerate(read(f).splitlines(), 1):
743:                 if f == NOTES and DECLARATION_LINE.match(line):
744:                     continue
745:                 for label, rx in FORBIDDEN_SHAPES:
746:                     if rx.search(line):
747:                         offenders.append(
748:                             f"{f.relative_to(REPO).as_posix()}:{lineno}: {label}")

982: ```python
983: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
984: # Group 3b: the real writers
985: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
986: def tree_paths(root):
987:     """Every FILE under root as a repo-relative normalized path, .git
988:     included. A SET OF PATHS, not contents: the mirror tool's status
989:     capture rewrites .git/index in place (new-review-mirror.ps1, the
990:     status capture), so a content diff would fire on a correct tool and
991:     a path-set diff does not."""
992:     return {norm(p.relative_to(root)) for p in root.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
993: 
994: 
995: def new_paths(root, before):
996:     return tree_paths(root) - before
997: 
998: 
999: def write_attestation(repo, base, head):
1000:     return subprocess.run(
1001:         [POWERSHELL, "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-File", str(ATTEST),
1002:          "-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-BaseSha", base, "-HeadSha", head,
1003:          "-Verdict", "PASS", "-VerificationStatus", "FULL",
1004:          "-RouteNote", "effective route confirmed", "-Rounds", "1",
1005:          "-Participants", "t (session) / t (reviewer)"],
1006:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
1007: 
1008: 
1009: @needs_host
1010: def test_the_real_writers_create_nothing_in_repo_but_the_attestation(tmp_path):
1011:     # The three tools that write during a round, run for real against a
1012:     # disposable two-commit repository. The only path that may appear
1013:     # inside the repository is the attestation, and it must satisfy the
1014:     # resolver's own membership answer.
1015:     from test_dispatch_round import build_real_mirror, prepare_default
1016:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path, name="src", commits=2)
1017:     base = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD~1").strip()
1018:     head = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD").strip()
1019:     before = tree_paths(repo)
1020: 
1021:     att = write_attestation(repo, base, head)
1022:     assert att.returncode == 0, att.stdout + att.stderr
1023:     mirror = build_real_mirror(tmp_path, source=repo)
1024:     assert norm(mirror.source) == norm(repo)
1025:     prep = prepare_default(tmp_path, mirror=mirror)
1026:     assert prep.returncode == 0, prep.stdout + prep.stderr
1027: 
1028:     appeared = new_paths(repo, before)
1029:     assert appeared == {f".git/parallax/attestations/{head}.json"}, sorted(appeared)
1030:     for rel in appeared:
1031:         proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", str(repo / rel))
1032:         assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1033:         assert "assert: inside attestation root" in proc.stdout
1034: 
1035: 
1036: @needs_host
1037: def test_a_writer_that_strays_is_reported(tmp_path):
1038:     # Negative control for the diff-and-assert logic above: a stub writer
1039:     # that lands a round record beside the plans root is caught by the
1040:     # same path-set diff and refused by the same membership answer.
1041:     repo = make_repo(tmp_path)
1042:     before = tree_paths(repo)
1043:     stray = repo / "rounds" / "x"
1044:     stray.parent.mkdir()
1045:     stray.write_text("a round record in the wrong root\n")
1046:     appeared = new_paths(repo, before)
1047:     assert appeared == {"rounds/x"}, sorted(appeared)
1048:     proc = run_resolver("-RepoRoot", str(repo), "-Assert", str(stray))
1049:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
1050:     assert "outside every retained root" in proc.stdout
1051: 
1052:

- Refuted, spec:18 "checkpoint root only inside write-attestation.ps1:99": also `tools/verify-attestation.ps1:125`, `tools/write-attestation.ps1:32`, and `references/application-checkpoint.md:80` (`<git-common-dir>/parallax/application-checkpoints/<stamp>-<reviewed-head12>.md`). This matters because the spec's static sweep (spec:198-199) scans `tools/` and must know its baseline.

**2. SDD ledger root is hard-coded by Superpowers.** ACCEPT. Read `C:/Users/Brandon/.claude/plugins/cache/claude-plugins-official/superpowers/6.3.0/skills/subagent-driven-development/scripts/sdd-workspace:35-39`: `base="$root/.superpowers/sdd"`, `dir="$base/$slug"`, `printf '*\n' > "$base/.gitignore"`, with `slug=$(basename "$plan" .md)` at `:31`. The spec's quoted path and the self-ignoring `.gitignore` match. The sentence "Its own ledger check reads that path back" (spec:26) is UNVERIFIED: I read only `sdd-workspace`, not `task-brief` or `review-package`.

**3. Eight `Canonical ... :` lines after the backup block are compatible with parsers and pin rules.** ACCEPT for the runtime parsers, REFUTE for the pin rules as the spec states them.
- Parsers are label-anchored and unaffected by lines that follow the backup block: `tools/check-drift.ps1:1010-1011`, `evals/tools/run_behavioral_evals.py:813-814`, `evals/tools/lane_credential_live_support.py:129` (`re.escape(label) + ": `([^`]+)`"`). Ordering pins end at the backup id: `test_multi_model_verify.py:1206-1210`, `test_backup_lane.py:80-81`, `test_seat_reshuffle.py:298-299`. No `count("Canonical ...")` pin exists in `evals/`.
- Pin rules: spec:194-196 says "A pin per line, in the `"literal" in body` form the coverage checker recognizes." That does not lock the region. `contract_coverage.py:131` folds the whole region into one normalized body, and `test_contract_coverage.py:537-553` lock that a pin stopping mid-region or two pins jointly spanning it leave the region uncovered. Eight per-line pins leave `artifact-roots` red under `test_every_marked_region_is_locked_by_a_pin` (`:951-954`). The fix is one pin holding the whole region text; the spec must also say exactly which text sits between the markers (only the eight lines, or the bullets at spec:88-108 too), because `CLAUDE.md` holds that a region too long for one pin is two regions.

**4. Existence-based override resolves KE, no false positive here or in fixtures.** ACCEPT. No `dev/` directory in this repo (Glob `dev/**` empty). No fixture in `evals/` creates one: the only `dev/docs` hits are pins on backup-lane prose (`test_backup_lane.py:692`); `test_dispatch_round.py:177-183` builds a one-file repo. KE resolution: `BACKLOG.md:100` records 76 gate folders under `dev/docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/`, so the directory exists there. Note that the rule fires on directory existence alone, so a consumer carrying both roots gets the override silently; the printed `docs-root source` line (spec:145-146) is what makes that visible.

**5. The `-Assert` set is right.** ACCEPT for the set, with one row-shape defect. The retention copy targets the rounds root (spec:170); nothing in the skill copies into the ledger or mirror. But the attestation and checkpoint rows are declared as `.git/parallax/...` (spec:84-85), while the tools resolve `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:54-63`, `:72`, `:99`; `tools/verify-attestation.ps1:125`, `:177`). In a linked worktree `.git` is a file and the common dir is elsewhere, so a resolver computing `<RepoRoot>/.git/parallax/attestations/` names a path the emitter never writes and `-Assert` on the real output answers `outside every retained root`. `references/application-checkpoint.md:80` already spells the row as `<git-common-dir>/...`; the declaration should match and the resolver should run the same `rev-parse`.

**6. The central assertion can FAIL and the fixtures fit.** ACCEPT that the fixtures write nothing in-repo; REFUTE that they fit the spec's description as written, and the can-fail property is asserted, not shown.
- Locations: source `tmp_path/mirror-src`, mirror `tmp_path/real-mirror` (`test_dispatch_round.py:177-188`); dispatch dir, receipt, body and prior all under `tmp_path` (`:230-235`, `:249-250`). Mirror sidecars land beside the mirror: `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1477-1478` (override file), `:743` (`.source-manifest`).
- Known in-repo write: git's index refresh during status capture rewrites `.git/index` (`tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1665-1669`). It modifies, it does not add a path, so the path-set diff at spec:224-225 survives it; a content diff would not.
- Fixture mismatch: `build_real_mirror` creates its own one-commit source (`:177-183`) and takes no source argument. `write-attestation.ps1:67-70` exits 2 when base and head resolve to the same commit. So the spec's "disposable repository with two commits ... through the build_real_mirror and prepare_default fixtures" (spec:208-222) cannot run without extending the fixture; adding a commit after the build moves `source_head` and `-Prepare` blocks on identity (`tools/dispatch-round.ps1:96-100`).
- Can-fail: no negative control is specified. `frozen-plan-format.md:22-25` requires every verification to be able to fail; the eval needs a stub writer that creates `<repo>/rounds/x` and an assertion that the diff logic reports it.
- Also: `test_dispatch_round.py:32-35` skips at module level; that mark does not travel with an import, so the new module needs its own.

**7. Sweep shapes catch the two KE roots, no false positive today.** ACCEPT on sufficiency for the two roots; REFUTE on "no false positive".
- Grep over `skills/ agents/ commands/ hooks/ tools/`: `superpowers/rounds` and `review-sources` have zero hits; all seven `docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/` hits are preceded by `plans/` (`debate-protocol.md:75`, `backup-lane.md:618`, `frozen-plan-format.md:85`, `model-prompting-notes.md:109,111`, `panels.md:63,95`). `dev/docs/superpowers` hits only `SKILL.md:323` and `frozen-plan-format.md:28`, both of which spec:171-173 removes.
- False positive: `references/model-prompting-notes.md:87` cites `.superpowers/sdd/2026-08-15-resume-preamble-refresh/progress.md`. Spec:205-207 defines a historical citation as a path under `docs/superpowers/plans/rounds/<date>-` only, so this dated ledger citation fails the sweep as specified. ## Findings the claims do not cover

**F1, blocking: preflight step 4 writes into the repo during the quiet period.** Spec:165-167 places the resolver as preflight step 4, after step 3, and retains `artifact-roots.txt` "in the rounds folder", which is inside the reviewed repo. Step 3 builds the mirror (`SKILL.md:58`; `preflight-mirror.md:3`). The contract region at `preflight-mirror.md:39-54` forbids any in-repo write from mirror build to wrapper exit and says one new untracked file is enough; `:59-60` says "BUILD THE MIRROR LAST. Every act that writes inside the reviewed repository finishes first." The first debate under the new text refuses at `-Prepare` (`dispatch-round.ps1:96-100`). Fix: run the resolver before the mirror build, write its output to scratch, and copy it into the rounds root after the wrapper exits, which is what spec:101-106 already says for every other retained artifact.

**F2, should-fix: SKILL.md has no room for a step 4.** `BACKLOG.md:4219-4227`: about 6485 of a 6500 hard ceiling, "The next edit that needs room in SKILL.md has to remove something." `evals/tools/skill_lint.py:132-133`, `:340-351` make over-ceiling an ERROR; CI tier 1 runs `--strict` (`skill-evals.yml:29-35`). The spec adds a multi-sentence step (spec:165-170) and a citation swap at `:322-324`, with no accounting. It must say where the words come from, or put the step body in a reference file with a one-line pointer.

**F3, should-fix:** attestation and checkpoint rows as `<git-common-dir>`-relative (claim 5).

**F4, should-fix:** one whole-region pin, and an exact statement of the region's contents (claim 3).

**F5, should-fix:** sweep exemption for dated `.superpowers/sdd/` citations (claim 7).

**F6, should-fix:** extend `build_real_mirror` to accept a prepared two-commit source, or state the fixture edit (claim 6).

**F7, should-fix:** a negative control for the tree-diff assertion (claim 6).

**F8, should-fix:** add the new module to `REQUIRED_DUAL_HOST_MODULES` and say so (claim 8).

**F9, note:** fact 1's three qualifiers ("once", "only", "both places") and the `SKILL.md:322` line number (claim 1). Correct in place.

**F10, note:** bare `rounds/` has no static-sweep shape (claim 7).

## Verdict

FIX, on blob `ed8674b2143d94ea5bea636ee3ed78e1e8acdfa7`. The design's core decisions (fixed ledger and mirror rows with reasons, existence-based override with `-DocsRoot`, the `-Assert` set, the exit map mirroring `dispatch-round.ps1:124-126`) are sound and evidenced. The spec cannot go to a plan as written because F1 makes the shipped skill contradict its own quiet-period contract on the first real debate, and F2 through F8 each describe an eval or an edit that would go red on a correct implementation or lock nothing. All are specific edits to the spec text; none reopens a decision.
